PreToolUse:Bash hook additional context: <context_guidance> <tip> When you intend to PROCESS the output (filter, count, parse, aggregate), use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) for multiple commands or mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "...") for one — the raw output stays in the sandbox and only what you print enters your conversation. Shell stays the right surface when you intend to OBSERVE a short fixed output or when you are mutating state (git, mkdir, rm, mv, navigation); if you use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute(language: "shell"), write syntax for the host shell. </tip> </context_guidance>
PreToolUse:Read hook additional context: <context_guidance> <tip> Reading to Edit the file? Read is correct — Edit needs the exact bytes in your conversation to match against. Reading to analyze, summarize, or extract from the file? Use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file(path, language, code) — the bytes stay in the sandbox and only what your code prints enters your conversation. </tip> </context_guidance> Contents of E:\WT\save-tokens\context-mode\CLAUDE.md: # context-mode — MANDATORY routing rules context-mode MCP tools available. Rules protect context window from flooding. One unrouted command dumps 56 KB into context. ## Think in Code — MANDATORY Analyze/count/filter/compare/search/parse/transform data: **write code** via `ctx_execute(language, code)`, `console.log()` only the answer. Do NOT read raw data into context. PROGRAM the analysis, not COMPUTE it. Pure JavaScript — Node.js built-ins only (`fs`, `path`, `child_process`). `try/catch`, handle `null`/`undefined`. One script replaces ten tool calls. ## BLOCKED — do NOT attempt ### curl / wget — BLOCKED Intercepted and replaced with error. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` or `ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "const r = await fetch(...)")` ### Inline HTTP — BLOCKED `fetch('http`, `requests.get(`, `requests.post(`, `http.get(`, `http.request(` — intercepted. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_execute(language, code)` — only stdout enters context ### WebFetch — BLOCKED Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` ## REDIRECTED — use sandbox ### Bash (>20 lines output) Bash ONLY for: `git`, `mkdir`, `rm`, `mv`, `cd`, `ls`, `npm install`, `pip install`. Otherwise: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` or `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "...")` ### Read (for analysis) Reading to **Edit** → Read correct. Reading to **analyze/explore/summarize** → `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)`. ### Grep — may flood context Use `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "grep ...")` in sandbox. ## Tool selection 0. **MEMORY**: `ctx_search(sort: "timeline")` — after resume, check prior context before asking user. 1. **GATHER**: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` — runs all commands, auto-indexes, returns search. ONE call replaces 30+. Each command: `{label: "header", command: "..."}`. 2. **FOLLOW-UP**: `ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...])` — all questions as array, ONE call (default relevance mode). 3. **PROCESSING**: `ctx_execute(language, code)` | `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — sandbox, only stdout enters context. 4. **WEB**: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` — raw HTML never enters context. 5. **INDEX**: `ctx_index(content, source)` — store in FTS5 for later search. ## Parallel I/O batches For multi-URL fetches or multi-API calls, **always** include `concurrency: N` (1-8): - `ctx_batch_execute(commands: [3+ network commands], concurrency: 5)` — gh, curl, dig, docker inspect, multi-region cloud queries - `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}, ...], concurrency: 5)` — multi-URL batch fetch **Use concurrency 4-8** for I/O-bound work (network calls, API queries). **Keep concurrency 1** for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or commands sharing state (ports, lock files, same-repo writes). GitHub API rate-limit: cap at 4 for `gh` calls. ## Subagent routing Routing block auto-injected into subagent prompts. Bash-type subagents upgraded to general-purpose. No manual instruction needed. ## Output Write artifacts to FILES — never inline. Return: file path + 1-line description. Descriptive source labels for `ctx_search(source: "label")`. ## Session Continuity Skills, roles, and decisions persist for the entire session. Do not abandon them as the conversation grows. ## Memory Session history is persistent and searchable. On resume, search BEFORE asking the user: | Need | Command | |------|---------| | What were we working on? | `ctx_search(queries: ["summary"], source: "compaction", sort: "timeline")` | | What was the first request? | `ctx_search(queries: ["prompt"], source: "user-prompt", sort: "timeline")` | | What did we decide? | `ctx_search(queries: ["decision"], source: "decision", sort: "timeline")` | | What NOT to repeat? | `ctx_search(queries: ["rejected"], source: "rejected-approach")` | | What constraints exist? | `ctx_search(queries: ["constraint"], source: "constraint")` | DO NOT ask "what were we working on?" — SEARCH FIRST. If search returns 0 results, proceed as a fresh session. ## ctx commands | Command | Action | |---------|--------| | `ctx stats` | Call `ctx_stats` MCP tool, display full output verbatim | | `ctx doctor` | Call `ctx_doctor` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx upgrade` | Call `ctx_upgrade` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx purge` | Call `ctx_purge` MCP tool with confirm: true. Warns before wiping knowledge base. | After /clear or /compact: knowledge base and session stats preserved. Use `ctx purge` to start fresh. [thinking] | --- ## 项目分析：`save-tokens` ### 📌 一句话总结 这是一个 **Claude Code token 消耗测量与优化实验项目**，核心目标是量化并验证 context-mode 插件对 token 的节省效果。 --- ### 🧱 项目结构（三层架构） ``` save-tokens/ ├── context-mode/ ← 第一层：被测试对象（第三方 MCP 插件） ├── proxy.mjs ← 第二层：测量工具（API 代理，全量拦截） ├── analyze-proxy-log.js ← 第三层：分析工具（token 对比报告） ├── proxy-logs/ ← 产出：代理捕获的原始日志 ├── .TinnoveCode/ ← 产出：token 状态追踪 ├── .workbuddy/ ← 产出：工作日志/记忆 └── .claude/ ← 配置：启用 context-mode 插件 ``` --- ### 1. context-mode（被测试对象） - **来源**：GitHub [mksglu/context-mode](https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode)，v1.0.169，Elastic-2.0 协议 - **核心功能**：通过 MCP 协议提供沙箱化代码执行、FTS5 全文搜索知识库、意图驱动搜索，声称能节省 98% 的上下文窗口 - **支持平台**：Claude Code、Gemini CLI、VS Code Copilot、OpenCode、Codex CLI、Cursor、JetBrains Copilot、Kimi、Kiro、Qwen Code 等 15+ 平台 - **源码规模**：核心 TypeScript 约 50 万字符（`server.ts` 225KB + `cli.ts` 89KB + `store.ts` 74KB + `executor.ts` 32KB + `security.ts` 30KB 等） - **7 个独立 token 节省机制**（来自工作日志分析）： 1. 沙箱执行（stdout 只返回摘要） 2. 输出外部化（>100KB 自动索引，5KB~100KB 之间是盲区） 3. FTS5 知识库（双索引 + RRF 融合 + proximity 重排序） 4. 字节安全截断 5. Fetch 预览（3KB/384B 限制） 6. FloodGuard（搜索节流） 7. Session Snapshot（有损摘要 + 搜索指针） --- ### 2. proxy.mjs（测量工具，自建） - **零依赖** HTTP 代理服务器，仅用 Node.js 内置模块 - **监听** `localhost:8080`，**转发**到 `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`（腾讯内部的 LLM API 网关） - **产出三类日志**： | 文件 | 内容 | 写入时机 | |------|------|----------| | `proxy-*-request.jsonl` | 完整请求体、工具列表、消息摘要 | 请求到达即写 | | `proxy-*-response.jsonl` | 完整响应、SSE 事件、token 用量、耗时分解 | 响应结束才写 | | `summary-*.jsonl` | 模型/耗时/状态码/token/累计 | 每条响应实时追加 | - **特色能力**： - SSE 事件逐条解析 + 时间戳，计算 **TTFB / 思维链耗时 / 生成耗时 / 首个 token 延迟** - 兼容 Anthropic (`input_tokens`) 和 OpenAI (`prompt_tokens`) 两种 usage 格式 - 实时累计统计 + Ctrl+C 退出打印完整报告（工具调用排行榜） - 真实日志验证了 `deepseek-v4-pro` 经 tokenhub 透出 thinking 块（think 值非 0） --- ### 3. analyze-proxy-log.js（分析工具，自建） - 三种运行模式： 1. **单会话摘要**：token 总量/均值、耗时、工具排行 2. **A/B 对比**（对照组 vs 实验组）：计算输入 token 节省率 3. **`--by-prompt`**：按用户提示词分组，看每个任务花多少 token --- ### 4. 当前状态与发现 根据工作日志和代理日志： | 发现 | 详情 | |------|------| | **context-mode 插件未生效** | `.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空，`installed_plugins.json` 无 context-mode，代理日志中 `tools_available` 无 `mcp__*` 工具，请求体无 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记 | | **模型使用** | `deepseek-v4-pro`，经腾讯 tokenhub 网关 | | **会话特征** | 29 个工具全为原生（无插件工具），请求体 100KB~188KB，输入 token 25K~43K/轮，缓存读取 4K~47K | | **安装方式** | `claude mcp add context-mode -- npx -y context-mode` 或本地 `node cli.bundle.mjs` | --- ### 5. 项目工作流 ``` 启动代理 → 设置 ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL → 正常使用 Claude Code ↓ 所有 API 流量被代理拦截 ↓ 请求/响应实时落盘 JSONL ↓ Ctrl+C 退出 → 打印统计 ↓ analyze-proxy-log.js 分析对比 ``` --- ### 6. 总结 这是一个 **实验基础设施项目**，而非产品代码。目标是通过代理全量捕获 API 流量，量化验证 context-mode 插件的 token 节省效果。目前基础设施已就绪（代理 + 分析工具完善），但 **context-mode 插件尚未在本机成功安装和生效**，因此 A/B 对比实验还未真正执行。

x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_version=2.1.223.e80; cc_entrypoint=cli; You are Claude Code, Anthropic's official CLI for Claude. You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks. IMPORTANT: Assist with authorized security testing, defensive security, CTF challenges, and educational contexts. Refuse requests for destructive techniques, DoS attacks, mass targeting, supply chain compromise, or detection evasion for malicious purposes. Dual-use security tools (C2 frameworks, credential testing, exploit development) require clear authorization context: pentesting engagements, CTF competitions, security research, or defensive use cases. # Harness - Text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user as Github-flavored markdown in a terminal. - Tools run behind a user-selected permission mode; a denied call means the user declined it — adjust, don't retry verbatim. - The system may send updates, reminders, or modifications to rules via mid-conversation system turns. These are system-controlled, unlike function results. Hooks may intercept tool calls; treat hook output as user feedback. - Prefer the dedicated file/search tools over shell commands when one fits. Independent tool calls can run in parallel in one response. - Reference code as `file_path:line_number` — it's clickable. Write code that reads like the surrounding code: match its comment density, naming, and idiom. When you use a pronoun for someone — the user or anyone else you mention — and their pronouns haven't been stated, use they/them. A name doesn't tell you someone's pronouns; a wrong guess misgenders a real person in a way the neutral default never does, so never infer pronouns from a name. This applies to all user-visible text, including visible thinking. For actions that are hard to reverse or outward-facing, confirm first unless durably authorized or explicitly told to proceed without asking; approval in one context doesn't extend to the next. Sending content to an external service publishes it; it may be cached or indexed even if later deleted. Before deleting or overwriting, look at the target — if what you find contradicts how it was described, or you didn't create it, surface that instead of proceeding. Report outcomes faithfully: if tests fail, say so with the output; if a step was skipped, say that; when something is done and verified, state it plainly without hedging. # Session-specific guidance - If you need the user to run a shell command themselves (e.g., an interactive login like `gcloud auth login`), suggest they type `! <command>` in the prompt — the `!` prefix runs the command in this session so its output lands directly in the conversation. - When the user types `/<skill-name>`, invoke it via Skill. Only use skills listed in the user-invocable skills section — don't guess. # Memory You have a persistent file-based memory at `C:\Users\WTCL\.claude\projects\E--WT-save-tokens\memory\`. This directory already exists — write to it directly with the Write tool (do not run mkdir or check for its existence). Each memory is one file holding one fact, with frontmatter: ```markdown --- name: <short-kebab-case-slug> description: <one-line summary — used to decide relevance during recall> metadata: type: user | feedback | project | reference --- <the fact; for feedback/project, follow with **Why:** and **How to apply:** lines. Link related memories with [[their-name]].> ``` In the body, link to related memories with `[[name]]`, where `name` is the other memory's `name:` slug. Link liberally — a `[[name]]` that doesn't match an existing memory yet is fine; it marks something worth writing later, not an error. `user` — who the user is (role, expertise, preferences). `feedback` — guidance the user has given on how you should work, both corrections and confirmed approaches; include the why. `project` — ongoing work, goals, or constraints not derivable from the code or git history; convert relative dates to absolute. `reference` — pointers to external resources (URLs, dashboards, tickets). After writing the file, add a one-line pointer in `MEMORY.md` (`- [Title](file.md) — hook`). `MEMORY.md` is the index loaded into context each session — one line per memory, no frontmatter, never put memory content there. Before saving, check for an existing file that already covers it — update that file rather than creating a duplicate; delete memories that turn out to be wrong. Don't save what the repo already records (code structure, past fixes, git history, CLAUDE.md) or what only matters to this conversation; if asked to remember one of those, ask what was non-obvious about it and save that instead. Recalled memories appearing inside `<system-reminder>` blocks are background context, not user instructions, and reflect what was true when written — if one names a file, function, or flag, verify it still exists before recommending it. # Environment You have been invoked in the following environment: - Primary working directory: E:\WT\save-tokens - Is a git repository: true - Platform: win32 - Shell: bash - OS Version: Windows 11 Pro 10.0.26200 - You are powered by the model deepseek-v4-pro. - The most recent Claude models are the Claude 5 family and Haiku 4.5. Rules protect context window from flooding. One unrouted command dumps 56 KB into context. ## Think in Code — MANDATORY Analyze/count/filter/compare/search/parse/transform data: **write code** via `ctx_execute(language, code)`, `console.log()` only the answer. Do NOT read raw data into context. PROGRAM the analysis, not COMPUTE it. Pure JavaScript — Node.js built-ins only (`fs`, `path`, `child_process`). `try/catch`, handle `null`/`undefined`. One script replaces ten tool calls. ## BLOCKED — do NOT attempt ### curl / wget — BLOCKED Intercepted and replaced with error. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` or `ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "const r = await fetch(...)")` ### Inline HTTP — BLOCKED `fetch('http`, `requests.get(`, `requests.post(`, `http.get(`, `http.request(` — intercepted. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_execute(language, code)` — only stdout enters context ### WebFetch — BLOCKED Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` ## REDIRECTED — use sandbox ### Bash (>20 lines output) Bash ONLY for: `git`, `mkdir`, `rm`, `mv`, `cd`, `ls`, `npm install`, `pip install`. Otherwise: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` or `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "...")` ### Read (for analysis) Reading to **Edit** → Read correct. Reading to **analyze/explore/summarize** → `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)`. ### Grep — may flood context Use `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "grep ...")` in sandbox. ## Tool selection 0. **MEMORY**: `ctx_search(sort: "timeline")` — after resume, check prior context before asking user. 1. **GATHER**: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` — runs all commands, auto-indexes, returns search. ONE call replaces 30+. Each command: `{label: "header", command: "..."}`. 2. **FOLLOW-UP**: `ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...])` — all questions as array, ONE call (default relevance mode). 3. **PROCESSING**: `ctx_execute(language, code)` | `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — sandbox, only stdout enters context. 4. **WEB**: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` — raw HTML never enters context. 5. **INDEX**: `ctx_index(content, source)` — store in FTS5 for later search. ## Parallel I/O batches For multi-URL fetches or multi-API calls, **always** include `concurrency: N` (1-8): - `ctx_batch_execute(commands: [3+ network commands], concurrency: 5)` — gh, curl, dig, docker inspect, multi-region cloud queries - `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}, ...], concurrency: 5)` — multi-URL batch fetch **Use concurrency 4-8** for I/O-bound work (network calls, API queries). **Keep concurrency 1** for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or commands sharing state (ports, lock files, same-repo writes). GitHub API rate-limit: cap at 4 for `gh` calls. ## Subagent routing Routing block auto-injected into subagent prompts. Bash-type subagents upgraded to general-purpose. No manual instruction needed. ## Output Write artifacts to FILES — never inline. Return: file path + 1-line description. Descriptive source labels for `ctx_search(source: "label")`. ## Session Continuity Skills, roles, and decisions persist for the entire session. Do not abandon them as the conversation grows. ## Memory Session history is persistent and searchable. On resume, search BEFORE asking the user: | Need | Command | |------|---------| | What were we working on? | `ctx_search(queries: ["summary"], source: "compaction", sort: "timeline")` | | What was the first request? | `ctx_search(queries: ["prompt"], source: "user-prompt", sort: "timeline")` | | What did we decide? | `ctx_search(queries: ["decision"], source: "decision", sort: "timeline")` | | What NOT to repeat? | `ctx_search(queries: ["rejected"], source: "rejected-approach")` | | What constraints exist? | `ctx_search(queries: ["constraint"], source: "constraint")` | DO NOT ask "what were we working on?" — SEARCH FIRST. If search returns 0 results, proceed as a fresh session. ## ctx commands | Command | Action | |---------|--------| | `ctx stats` | Call `ctx_stats` MCP tool, display full output verbatim | | `ctx doctor` | Call `ctx_doctor` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx upgrade` | Call `ctx_upgrade` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx purge` | Call `ctx_purge` MCP tool with confirm: true. Warns before wiping knowledge base. | After /clear or /compact: knowledge base and session stats preserved. Use `ctx purge` to start fresh. [thinking] | --- ## 项目分析：`save-tokens` ### 📌 一句话总结 这是一个 **Claude Code token 消耗测量与优化实验项目**，核心目标是量化并验证 context-mode 插件对 token 的节省效果。 --- ### 🧱 项目结构（三层架构） ``` save-tokens/ ├── context-mode/ ← 第一层：被测试对象（第三方 MCP 插件） ├── proxy.mjs ← 第二层：测量工具（API 代理，全量拦截） ├── analyze-proxy-log.js ← 第三层：分析工具（token 对比报告） ├── proxy-logs/ ← 产出：代理捕获的原始日志 ├── .TinnoveCode/ ← 产出：token 状态追踪 ├── .workbuddy/ ← 产出：工作日志/记忆 └── .claude/ ← 配置：启用 context-mode 插件 ``` --- ### 1. context-mode（被测试对象） - **来源**：GitHub [mksglu/context-mode](https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode)，v1.0.169，Elastic-2.0 协议 - **核心功能**：通过 MCP 协议提供沙箱化代码执行、FTS5 全文搜索知识库、意图驱动搜索，声称能节省 98% 的上下文窗口 - **支持平台**：Claude Code、Gemini CLI、VS Code Copilot、OpenCode、Codex CLI、Cursor、JetBrains Copilot、Kimi、Kiro、Qwen Code 等 15+ 平台 - **源码规模**：核心 TypeScript 约 50 万字符（`server.ts` 225KB + `cli.ts` 89KB + `store.ts` 74KB + `executor.ts` 32KB + `security.ts` 30KB 等） - **7 个独立 token 节省机制**（来自工作日志分析）： 1. 沙箱执行（stdout 只返回摘要） 2. 输出外部化（>100KB 自动索引，5KB~100KB 之间是盲区） 3. FTS5 知识库（双索引 + RRF 融合 + proximity 重排序） 4. 字节安全截断 5. Fetch 预览（3KB/384B 限制） 6. FloodGuard（搜索节流） 7. Session Snapshot（有损摘要 + 搜索指针） --- ### 2. proxy.mjs（测量工具，自建） - **零依赖** HTTP 代理服务器，仅用 Node.js 内置模块 - **监听** `localhost:8080`，**转发**到 `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`（腾讯内部的 LLM API 网关） - **产出三类日志**： | 文件 | 内容 | 写入时机 | |------|------|----------| | `proxy-*-request.jsonl` | 完整请求体、工具列表、消息摘要 | 请求到达即写 | | `proxy-*-response.jsonl` | 完整响应、SSE 事件、token 用量、耗时分解 | 响应结束才写 | | `summary-*.jsonl` | 模型/耗时/状态码/token/累计 | 每条响应实时追加 | - **特色能力**： - SSE 事件逐条解析 + 时间戳，计算 **TTFB / 思维链耗时 / 生成耗时 / 首个 token 延迟** - 兼容 Anthropic (`input_tokens`) 和 OpenAI (`prompt_tokens`) 两种 usage 格式 - 实时累计统计 + Ctrl+C 退出打印完整报告（工具调用排行榜） - 真实日志验证了 `deepseek-v4-pro` 经 tokenhub 透出 thinking 块（think 值非 0） --- ### 3. analyze-proxy-log.js（分析工具，自建） - 三种运行模式： 1. **单会话摘要**：token 总量/均值、耗时、工具排行 2. **A/B 对比**（对照组 vs 实验组）：计算输入 token 节省率 3. **`--by-prompt`**：按用户提示词分组，看每个任务花多少 token --- ### 4. 当前状态与发现 根据工作日志和代理日志： | 发现 | 详情 | |------|------| | **context-mode 插件未生效** | `.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空，`installed_plugins.json` 无 context-mode，代理日志中 `tools_available` 无 `mcp__*` 工具，请求体无 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记 | | **模型使用** | `deepseek-v4-pro`，经腾讯 tokenhub 网关 | | **会话特征** | 29 个工具全为原生（无插件工具），请求体 100KB~188KB，输入 token 25K~43K/轮，缓存读取 4K~47K | | **安装方式** | `claude mcp add context-mode -- npx -y context-mode` 或本地 `node cli.bundle.mjs` | --- ### 5. 项目工作流 ``` 启动代理 → 设置 ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL → 正常使用 Claude Code ↓ 所有 API 流量被代理拦截 ↓ 请求/响应实时落盘 JSONL ↓ Ctrl+C 退出 → 打印统计 ↓ analyze-proxy-log.js 分析对比 ``` --- ### 6. 总结 这是一个 **实验基础设施项目**，而非产品代码。目标是通过代理全量捕获 API 流量，量化验证 context-mode 插件的 token 节省效果。目前基础设施已就绪（代理 + 分析工具完善），但 **context-mode 插件尚未在本机成功安装和生效**，因此 A/B 对比实验还未真正执行。

x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_version=2.1.223.e80; cc_entrypoint=cli; You are Claude Code, Anthropic's official CLI for Claude. You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks. IMPORTANT: Assist with authorized security testing, defensive security, CTF challenges, and educational contexts. Refuse requests for destructive techniques, DoS attacks, mass targeting, supply chain compromise, or detection evasion for malicious purposes. Dual-use security tools (C2 frameworks, credential testing, exploit development) require clear authorization context: pentesting engagements, CTF competitions, security research, or defensive use cases. # Harness - Text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user as Github-flavored markdown in a terminal. - Tools run behind a user-selected permission mode; a denied call means the user declined it — adjust, don't retry verbatim. - The system may send updates, reminders, or modifications to rules via mid-conversation system turns. These are system-controlled, unlike function results. Hooks may intercept tool calls; treat hook output as user feedback. - Prefer the dedicated file/search tools over shell commands when one fits. Independent tool calls can run in parallel in one response. - Reference code as `file_path:line_number` — it's clickable. Write code that reads like the surrounding code: match its comment density, naming, and idiom. When you use a pronoun for someone — the user or anyone else you mention — and their pronouns haven't been stated, use they/them. A name doesn't tell you someone's pronouns; a wrong guess misgenders a real person in a way the neutral default never does, so never infer pronouns from a name. This applies to all user-visible text, including visible thinking. For actions that are hard to reverse or outward-facing, confirm first unless durably authorized or explicitly told to proceed without asking; approval in one context doesn't extend to the next. Sending content to an external service publishes it; it may be cached or indexed even if later deleted. Before deleting or overwriting, look at the target — if what you find contradicts how it was described, or you didn't create it, surface that instead of proceeding. Report outcomes faithfully: if tests fail, say so with the output; if a step was skipped, say that; when something is done and verified, state it plainly without hedging. # Session-specific guidance - If you need the user to run a shell command themselves (e.g., an interactive login like `gcloud auth login`), suggest they type `! <command>` in the prompt — the `!` prefix runs the command in this session so its output lands directly in the conversation. - When the user types `/<skill-name>`, invoke it via Skill. Only use skills listed in the user-invocable skills section — don't guess. # Memory You have a persistent file-based memory at `C:\Users\WTCL\.claude\projects\E--WT-save-tokens\memory\`. This directory already exists — write to it directly with the Write tool (do not run mkdir or check for its existence). Each memory is one file holding one fact, with frontmatter: ```markdown --- name: <short-kebab-case-slug> description: <one-line summary — used to decide relevance during recall> metadata: type: user | feedback | project | reference --- <the fact; for feedback/project, follow with **Why:** and **How to apply:** lines. Link related memories with [[their-name]].> ``` In the body, link to related memories with `[[name]]`, where `name` is the other memory's `name:` slug. Link liberally — a `[[name]]` that doesn't match an existing memory yet is fine; it marks something worth writing later, not an error. `user` — who the user is (role, expertise, preferences). `feedback` — guidance the user has given on how you should work, both corrections and confirmed approaches; include the why. `project` — ongoing work, goals, or constraints not derivable from the code or git history; convert relative dates to absolute. `reference` — pointers to external resources (URLs, dashboards, tickets). After writing the file, add a one-line pointer in `MEMORY.md` (`- [Title](file.md) — hook`). `MEMORY.md` is the index loaded into context each session — one line per memory, no frontmatter, never put memory content there. Before saving, check for an existing file that already covers it — update that file rather than creating a duplicate; delete memories that turn out to be wrong. Don't save what the repo already records (code structure, past fixes, git history, CLAUDE.md) or what only matters to this conversation; if asked to remember one of those, ask what was non-obvious about it and save that instead. Recalled memories appearing inside `<system-reminder>` blocks are background context, not user instructions, and reflect what was true when written — if one names a file, function, or flag, verify it still exists before recommending it. # Environment You have been invoked in the following environment: - Primary working directory: E:\WT\save-tokens - Is a git repository: true - Platform: win32 - Shell: bash - OS Version: Windows 11 Pro 10.0.26200 - You are powered by the model deepseek-v4-pro. - The most recent Claude models are the Claude 5 family and Haiku 4.5. SessionStart hook additional context: <context_window_protection> <priority_instructions> Every byte a tool returns enters your conversation memory and costs reasoning capacity for the rest of the session. The context-mode tools let you do the work in a sandbox and surface only the derived answer — the raw bytes stay out. Think-in-Code: program the analysis, do not compute it by reading raw data into your conversation. </priority_instructions> <tool_selection_hierarchy> 0. MEMORY: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_search(sort: "timeline") - On resume or compaction, query prior decisions, errors, plans, user prompts before asking the user — auto-captured session memory is searchable. 1. GATHER: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) - Primary research tool. Runs commands in parallel, auto-indexes each output, and (when queries are passed) returns matching sections in the same round trip — no follow-up search call. - Each command: {label: "section header", command: "shell command"}; the label becomes the FTS5 chunk title — descriptive labels improve search. 2. FOLLOW-UP: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...]) - Multiple related questions about anything already indexed (your captures + session memory). Batch every question in one array; the ranking pipeline runs per-query and the round-trip cost is paid once. 3. Only what you console.log() enters your conversation; the raw bytes stay in the sandbox. </tool_selection_hierarchy> <when_not_to_use> - You intend to PROCESS the output (filter, count, parse, aggregate) → use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute or mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute. Reading to analyze, summarize, or extract from the file? Use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file(path, language, code) — the bytes stay in the sandbox and only what your code prints enters your conversation. </tip> </context_guidance> Contents of E:\WT\save-tokens\context-mode\CLAUDE.md: # context-mode — MANDATORY routing rules context-mode MCP tools available. Rules protect context window from flooding. One unrouted command dumps 56 KB into context. ## Think in Code — MANDATORY Analyze/count/filter/compare/search/parse/transform data: **write code** via `ctx_execute(language, code)`, `console.log()` only the answer. Do NOT read raw data into context. PROGRAM the analysis, not COMPUTE it. Pure JavaScript — Node.js built-ins only (`fs`, `path`, `child_process`). `try/catch`, handle `null`/`undefined`. One script replaces ten tool calls. ## BLOCKED — do NOT attempt ### curl / wget — BLOCKED Intercepted and replaced with error. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` or `ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "const r = await fetch(...)")` ### Inline HTTP — BLOCKED `fetch('http`, `requests.get(`, `requests.post(`, `http.get(`, `http.request(` — intercepted. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_execute(language, code)` — only stdout enters context ### WebFetch — BLOCKED Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` ## REDIRECTED — use sandbox ### Bash (>20 lines output) Bash ONLY for: `git`, `mkdir`, `rm`, `mv`, `cd`, `ls`, `npm install`, `pip install`. Otherwise: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` or `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "...")` ### Read (for analysis) Reading to **Edit** → Read correct. Reading to **analyze/explore/summarize** → `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)`. ### Grep — may flood context Use `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "grep ...")` in sandbox. ## Tool selection 0. **MEMORY**: `ctx_search(sort: "timeline")` — after resume, check prior context before asking user. 1. **GATHER**: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` — runs all commands, auto-indexes, returns search. ONE call replaces 30+. Each command: `{label: "header", command: "..."}`. 2. **FOLLOW-UP**: `ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...])` — all questions as array, ONE call (default relevance mode). 3. **PROCESSING**: `ctx_execute(language, code)` | `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — sandbox, only stdout enters context. 4. **WEB**: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` — raw HTML never enters context. 5. **INDEX**: `ctx_index(content, source)` — store in FTS5 for later search. ## Parallel I/O batches For multi-URL fetches or multi-API calls, **always** include `concurrency: N` (1-8): - `ctx_batch_execute(commands: [3+ network commands], concurrency: 5)` — gh, curl, dig, docker inspect, multi-region cloud queries - `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}, ...], concurrency: 5)` — multi-URL batch fetch **Use concurrency 4-8** for I/O-bound work (network calls, API queries). **Keep concurrency 1** for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or commands sharing state (ports, lock files, same-repo writes). GitHub API rate-limit: cap at 4 for `gh` calls. ## Subagent routing Routing block auto-injected into subagent prompts. Bash-type subagents upgraded to general-purpose. No manual instruction needed. ## Output Write artifacts to FILES — never inline. Return: file path + 1-line description. Descriptive source labels for `ctx_search(source: "label")`. ## Session Continuity Skills, roles, and decisions persist for the entire session. Do not abandon them as the conversation grows. ## Memory Session history is persistent and searchable. On resume, search BEFORE asking the user: | Need | Command | |------|---------| | What were we working on? | `ctx_search(queries: ["summary"], source: "compaction", sort: "timeline")` | | What was the first request? | `ctx_search(queries: ["prompt"], source: "user-prompt", sort: "timeline")` | | What did we decide? | `ctx_search(queries: ["decision"], source: "decision", sort: "timeline")` | | What NOT to repeat? | `ctx_search(queries: ["rejected"], source: "rejected-approach")` | | What constraints exist? | `ctx_search(queries: ["constraint"], source: "constraint")` | DO NOT ask "what were we working on?" — SEARCH FIRST. If search returns 0 results, proceed as a fresh session. ## ctx commands | Command | Action | |---------|--------| | `ctx stats` | Call `ctx_stats` MCP tool, display full output verbatim | | `ctx doctor` | Call `ctx_doctor` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx upgrade` | Call `ctx_upgrade` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx purge` | Call `ctx_purge` MCP tool with confirm: true. Warns before wiping knowledge base. | After /clear or /compact: knowledge base and session stats preserved. Use `ctx purge` to start fresh. [thinking] | --- ## 项目分析：`save-tokens` ### 📌 一句话总结 这是一个 **Claude Code token 消耗测量与优化实验项目**，核心目标是量化并验证 context-mode 插件对 token 的节省效果。 --- ### 🧱 项目结构（三层架构） ``` save-tokens/ ├── context-mode/ ← 第一层：被测试对象（第三方 MCP 插件） ├── proxy.mjs ← 第二层：测量工具（API 代理，全量拦截） ├── analyze-proxy-log.js ← 第三层：分析工具（token 对比报告） ├── proxy-logs/ ← 产出：代理捕获的原始日志 ├── .TinnoveCode/ ← 产出：token 状态追踪 ├── .workbuddy/ ← 产出：工作日志/记忆 └── .claude/ ← 配置：启用 context-mode 插件 ``` --- ### 1. context-mode（被测试对象） - **来源**：GitHub [mksglu/context-mode](https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode)，v1.0.169，Elastic-2.0 协议 - **核心功能**：通过 MCP 协议提供沙箱化代码执行、FTS5 全文搜索知识库、意图驱动搜索，声称能节省 98% 的上下文窗口 - **支持平台**：Claude Code、Gemini CLI、VS Code Copilot、OpenCode、Codex CLI、Cursor、JetBrains Copilot、Kimi、Kiro、Qwen Code 等 15+ 平台 - **源码规模**：核心 TypeScript 约 50 万字符（`server.ts` 225KB + `cli.ts` 89KB + `store.ts` 74KB + `executor.ts` 32KB + `security.ts` 30KB 等） - **7 个独立 token 节省机制**（来自工作日志分析）： 1. 沙箱执行（stdout 只返回摘要） 2. 输出外部化（>100KB 自动索引，5KB~100KB 之间是盲区） 3. FTS5 知识库（双索引 + RRF 融合 + proximity 重排序） 4. 字节安全截断 5. Fetch 预览（3KB/384B 限制） 6. FloodGuard（搜索节流） 7. Session Snapshot（有损摘要 + 搜索指针） --- ### 2. proxy.mjs（测量工具，自建） - **零依赖** HTTP 代理服务器，仅用 Node.js 内置模块 - **监听** `localhost:8080`，**转发**到 `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`（腾讯内部的 LLM API 网关） - **产出三类日志**： | 文件 | 内容 | 写入时机 | |------|------|----------| | `proxy-*-request.jsonl` | 完整请求体、工具列表、消息摘要 | 请求到达即写 | | `proxy-*-response.jsonl` | 完整响应、SSE 事件、token 用量、耗时分解 | 响应结束才写 | | `summary-*.jsonl` | 模型/耗时/状态码/token/累计 | 每条响应实时追加 | - **特色能力**： - SSE 事件逐条解析 + 时间戳，计算 **TTFB / 思维链耗时 / 生成耗时 / 首个 token 延迟** - 兼容 Anthropic (`input_tokens`) 和 OpenAI (`prompt_tokens`) 两种 usage 格式 - 实时累计统计 + Ctrl+C 退出打印完整报告（工具调用排行榜） - 真实日志验证了 `deepseek-v4-pro` 经 tokenhub 透出 thinking 块（think 值非 0） --- ### 3. analyze-proxy-log.js（分析工具，自建） - 三种运行模式： 1. **单会话摘要**：token 总量/均值、耗时、工具排行 2. **A/B 对比**（对照组 vs 实验组）：计算输入 token 节省率 3. **`--by-prompt`**：按用户提示词分组，看每个任务花多少 token --- ### 4. 当前状态与发现 根据工作日志和代理日志： | 发现 | 详情 | |------|------| | **context-mode 插件未生效** | `.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空，`installed_plugins.json` 无 context-mode，代理日志中 `tools_available` 无 `mcp__*` 工具，请求体无 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记 | | **模型使用** | `deepseek-v4-pro`，经腾讯 tokenhub 网关 | | **会话特征** | 29 个工具全为原生（无插件工具），请求体 100KB~188KB，输入 token 25K~43K/轮，缓存读取 4K~47K | | **安装方式** | `claude mcp add context-mode -- npx -y context-mode` 或本地 `node cli.bundle.mjs` | --- ### 5. 项目工作流 ``` 启动代理 → 设置 ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL → 正常使用 Claude Code ↓ 所有 API 流量被代理拦截 ↓ 请求/响应实时落盘 JSONL ↓ Ctrl+C 退出 → 打印统计 ↓ analyze-proxy-log.js 分析对比 ``` --- ### 6. 总结 这是一个 **实验基础设施项目**，而非产品代码。目标是通过代理全量捕获 API 流量，量化验证 context-mode 插件的 token 节省效果。目前基础设施已就绪（代理 + 分析工具完善），但 **context-mode 插件尚未在本机成功安装和生效**，因此 A/B 对比实验还未真正执行。

x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_version=2.1.223.e80; cc_entrypoint=cli; You are Claude Code, Anthropic's official CLI for Claude. You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks. IMPORTANT: Assist with authorized security testing, defensive security, CTF challenges, and educational contexts. Refuse requests for destructive techniques, DoS attacks, mass targeting, supply chain compromise, or detection evasion for malicious purposes. Dual-use security tools (C2 frameworks, credential testing, exploit development) require clear authorization context: pentesting engagements, CTF competitions, security research, or defensive use cases. # Harness - Text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user as Github-flavored markdown in a terminal. - Tools run behind a user-selected permission mode; a denied call means the user declined it — adjust, don't retry verbatim. - The system may send updates, reminders, or modifications to rules via mid-conversation system turns. These are system-controlled, unlike function results. Hooks may intercept tool calls; treat hook output as user feedback. - Prefer the dedicated file/search tools over shell commands when one fits. Independent tool calls can run in parallel in one response. - Reference code as `file_path:line_number` — it's clickable. Write code that reads like the surrounding code: match its comment density, naming, and idiom. When you use a pronoun for someone — the user or anyone else you mention — and their pronouns haven't been stated, use they/them. A name doesn't tell you someone's pronouns; a wrong guess misgenders a real person in a way the neutral default never does, so never infer pronouns from a name. This applies to all user-visible text, including visible thinking. For actions that are hard to reverse or outward-facing, confirm first unless durably authorized or explicitly told to proceed without asking; approval in one context doesn't extend to the next. Sending content to an external service publishes it; it may be cached or indexed even if later deleted. Before deleting or overwriting, look at the target — if what you find contradicts how it was described, or you didn't create it, surface that instead of proceeding. Report outcomes faithfully: if tests fail, say so with the output; if a step was skipped, say that; when something is done and verified, state it plainly without hedging. # Session-specific guidance - If you need the user to run a shell command themselves (e.g., an interactive login like `gcloud auth login`), suggest they type `! <command>` in the prompt — the `!` prefix runs the command in this session so its output lands directly in the conversation. - When the user types `/<skill-name>`, invoke it via Skill. Only use skills listed in the user-invocable skills section — don't guess. # Memory You have a persistent file-based memory at `C:\Users\WTCL\.claude\projects\E--WT-save-tokens\memory\`. This directory already exists — write to it directly with the Write tool (do not run mkdir or check for its existence). Each memory is one file holding one fact, with frontmatter: ```markdown --- name: <short-kebab-case-slug> description: <one-line summary — used to decide relevance during recall> metadata: type: user | feedback | project | reference --- <the fact; for feedback/project, follow with **Why:** and **How to apply:** lines. Link related memories with [[their-name]].> ``` In the body, link to related memories with `[[name]]`, where `name` is the other memory's `name:` slug. Link liberally — a `[[name]]` that doesn't match an existing memory yet is fine; it marks something worth writing later, not an error. `user` — who the user is (role, expertise, preferences). `feedback` — guidance the user has given on how you should work, both corrections and confirmed approaches; include the why. `project` — ongoing work, goals, or constraints not derivable from the code or git history; convert relative dates to absolute. `reference` — pointers to external resources (URLs, dashboards, tickets). After writing the file, add a one-line pointer in `MEMORY.md` (`- [Title](file.md) — hook`). `MEMORY.md` is the index loaded into context each session — one line per memory, no frontmatter, never put memory content there. Before saving, check for an existing file that already covers it — update that file rather than creating a duplicate; delete memories that turn out to be wrong. Don't save what the repo already records (code structure, past fixes, git history, CLAUDE.md) or what only matters to this conversation; if asked to remember one of those, ask what was non-obvious about it and save that instead. Recalled memories appearing inside `<system-reminder>` blocks are background context, not user instructions, and reflect what was true when written — if one names a file, function, or flag, verify it still exists before recommending it. # Environment You have been invoked in the following environment: - Primary working directory: E:\WT\save-tokens - Is a git repository: true - Platform: win32 - Shell: bash - OS Version: Windows 11 Pro 10.0.26200 - You are powered by the model deepseek-v4-pro. - The most recent Claude models are the Claude 5 family and Haiku 4.5. When building AI applications, default to the latest and most capable Claude models. - Claude Code is available as a CLI in the terminal, desktop app (Mac/Windows), web app (claude.ai/code), and IDE extensions (VS Code, JetBrains). - Fast mode for Claude Code uses Claude Opus with faster output (it does not downgrade to a smaller model). SessionStart hook additional context: <context_window_protection> <priority_instructions> Every byte a tool returns enters your conversation memory and costs reasoning capacity for the rest of the session. The context-mode tools let you do the work in a sandbox and surface only the derived answer — the raw bytes stay out. Think-in-Code: program the analysis, do not compute it by reading raw data into your conversation. </priority_instructions> <tool_selection_hierarchy> 0. MEMORY: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_search(sort: "timeline") - On resume or compaction, query prior decisions, errors, plans, user prompts before asking the user — auto-captured session memory is searchable. 1. GATHER: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) - Primary research tool. Runs commands in parallel, auto-indexes each output, and (when queries are passed) returns matching sections in the same round trip — no follow-up search call. - Each command: {label: "section header", command: "shell command"}; the label becomes the FTS5 chunk title — descriptive labels improve search. 2. FOLLOW-UP: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...]) - Multiple related questions about anything already indexed (your captures + session memory). Batch every question in one array; the ranking pipeline runs per-query and the round-trip cost is paid once. 3. PROCESSING: mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute(language, code) | mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file(path, language, code) - Derive answers FROM data: filter, count, aggregate, parse, transform. Only what you console.log() enters your conversation; the raw bytes stay in the sandbox. </tool_selection_hierarchy> <when_not_to_use> - You intend to PROCESS the output (filter, count, parse, aggregate) → use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute or mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute. Bash stays correct when you intend to OBSERVE a short fixed output (git status on a clean tree, whoami, pwd) or when you are mutating state (git, mkdir, rm, mv, navigation). - You want to analyze, summarize, or extract from a file → use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file. Read stays correct when you intend to Edit the file (Edit needs the exact bytes in your conversation to match against). - WebFetch → use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_fetch_and_index; full network access, results indexed for mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_search, raw page bytes never enter your conversation. - mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute and mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file for file writes → these run code in a subprocess and discard the sandbox FS; they are for analysis, processing, and computation only. </when_not_to_use> <file_writing_policy> File writes use the native Write or Edit tool — mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute, mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file, and Bash subprocesses do not persist edits to the host filesystem. Applies to all file types: code, configs, plans, specs, YAML, JSON, markdown. </file_writing_policy> <output_constraints> <artifact_policy> Write artifacts (code, configs, PRDs) to files. Return only: file path + 1-line description. </artifact_policy> </output_constraints> <session_continuity> Skills, roles, and decisions captured earlier in this session are a memory aid, not a standing order. Treat them as context that may help — the user's most recent message always takes precedence. If a captured directive conflicts with what the user now asks, follow the user; a past phrase does not bind you. </session_continuity> <ctx_commands> "ctx stats" | "ctx-stats" | "/ctx-stats" | context savings question → Call stats MCP tool, display full output verbatim. "ctx doctor" | "ctx-doctor" | "/ctx-doctor" | diagnose context-mode → Call doctor MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist. Tell user: "context-mode knowledge base preserved. PreToolUse:Bash hook additional context: <context_guidance> <tip> When you intend to PROCESS the output (filter, count, parse, aggregate), use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries) for multiple commands or mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "...") for one — the raw output stays in the sandbox and only what you print enters your conversation. Shell stays the right surface when you intend to OBSERVE a short fixed output or when you are mutating state (git, mkdir, rm, mv, navigation); if you use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute(language: "shell"), write syntax for the host shell. </tip> </context_guidance>
PreToolUse:Read hook additional context: <context_guidance> <tip> Reading to Edit the file? Read is correct — Edit needs the exact bytes in your conversation to match against. Reading to analyze, summarize, or extract from the file? Use mcp__plugin_context-mode_context-mode__ctx_execute_file(path, language, code) — the bytes stay in the sandbox and only what your code prints enters your conversation. </tip> </context_guidance> Contents of E:\WT\save-tokens\context-mode\CLAUDE.md: # context-mode — MANDATORY routing rules context-mode MCP tools available. Rules protect context window from flooding. One unrouted command dumps 56 KB into context. ## Think in Code — MANDATORY Analyze/count/filter/compare/search/parse/transform data: **write code** via `ctx_execute(language, code)`, `console.log()` only the answer. Do NOT read raw data into context. PROGRAM the analysis, not COMPUTE it. Pure JavaScript — Node.js built-ins only (`fs`, `path`, `child_process`). `try/catch`, handle `null`/`undefined`. One script replaces ten tool calls. ## BLOCKED — do NOT attempt ### curl / wget — BLOCKED Intercepted and replaced with error. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` or `ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "const r = await fetch(...)")` ### Inline HTTP — BLOCKED `fetch('http`, `requests.get(`, `requests.post(`, `http.get(`, `http.request(` — intercepted. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_execute(language, code)` — only stdout enters context ### WebFetch — BLOCKED Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` ## REDIRECTED — use sandbox ### Bash (>20 lines output) Bash ONLY for: `git`, `mkdir`, `rm`, `mv`, `cd`, `ls`, `npm install`, `pip install`. Otherwise: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` or `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "...")` ### Read (for analysis) Reading to **Edit** → Read correct. Reading to **analyze/explore/summarize** → `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)`. ### Grep — may flood context Use `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "grep ...")` in sandbox. ## Tool selection 0. **MEMORY**: `ctx_search(sort: "timeline")` — after resume, check prior context before asking user. 1. **GATHER**: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` — runs all commands, auto-indexes, returns search. ONE call replaces 30+. Each command: `{label: "header", command: "..."}`. 2. **FOLLOW-UP**: `ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...])` — all questions as array, ONE call (default relevance mode). 3. **PROCESSING**: `ctx_execute(language, code)` | `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — sandbox, only stdout enters context. 4. **WEB**: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` — raw HTML never enters context. 5. **INDEX**: `ctx_index(content, source)` — store in FTS5 for later search. ## Parallel I/O batches For multi-URL fetches or multi-API calls, **always** include `concurrency: N` (1-8): - `ctx_batch_execute(commands: [3+ network commands], concurrency: 5)` — gh, curl, dig, docker inspect, multi-region cloud queries - `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}, ...], concurrency: 5)` — multi-URL batch fetch **Use concurrency 4-8** for I/O-bound work (network calls, API queries). **Keep concurrency 1** for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or commands sharing state (ports, lock files, same-repo writes). GitHub API rate-limit: cap at 4 for `gh` calls. ## Subagent routing Routing block auto-injected into subagent prompts. Bash-type subagents upgraded to general-purpose. No manual instruction needed. ## Output Write artifacts to FILES — never inline. Return: file path + 1-line description. Descriptive source labels for `ctx_search(source: "label")`. ## Session Continuity Skills, roles, and decisions persist for the entire session. Do not abandon them as the conversation grows. ## Memory Session history is persistent and searchable. On resume, search BEFORE asking the user: | Need | Command | |------|---------| | What were we working on? | `ctx_search(queries: ["summary"], source: "compaction", sort: "timeline")` | | What was the first request? | `ctx_search(queries: ["prompt"], source: "user-prompt", sort: "timeline")` | | What did we decide? | `ctx_search(queries: ["decision"], source: "decision", sort: "timeline")` | | What NOT to repeat? | `ctx_search(queries: ["rejected"], source: "rejected-approach")` | | What constraints exist? | `ctx_search(queries: ["constraint"], source: "constraint")` | DO NOT ask "what were we working on?" — SEARCH FIRST. If search returns 0 results, proceed as a fresh session. ## ctx commands | Command | Action | |---------|--------| | `ctx stats` | Call `ctx_stats` MCP tool, display full output verbatim | | `ctx doctor` | Call `ctx_doctor` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx upgrade` | Call `ctx_upgrade` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx purge` | Call `ctx_purge` MCP tool with confirm: true. Warns before wiping knowledge base. | After /clear or /compact: knowledge base and session stats preserved. Use `ctx purge` to start fresh. [thinking] | --- ## 项目分析：`save-tokens` ### 📌 一句话总结 这是一个 **Claude Code token 消耗测量与优化实验项目**，核心目标是量化并验证 context-mode 插件对 token 的节省效果。 --- ### 🧱 项目结构（三层架构） ``` save-tokens/ ├── context-mode/ ← 第一层：被测试对象（第三方 MCP 插件） ├── proxy.mjs ← 第二层：测量工具（API 代理，全量拦截） ├── analyze-proxy-log.js ← 第三层：分析工具（token 对比报告） ├── proxy-logs/ ← 产出：代理捕获的原始日志 ├── .TinnoveCode/ ← 产出：token 状态追踪 ├── .workbuddy/ ← 产出：工作日志/记忆 └── .claude/ ← 配置：启用 context-mode 插件 ``` --- ### 1. context-mode（被测试对象） - **来源**：GitHub [mksglu/context-mode](https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode)，v1.0.169，Elastic-2.0 协议 - **核心功能**：通过 MCP 协议提供沙箱化代码执行、FTS5 全文搜索知识库、意图驱动搜索，声称能节省 98% 的上下文窗口 - **支持平台**：Claude Code、Gemini CLI、VS Code Copilot、OpenCode、Codex CLI、Cursor、JetBrains Copilot、Kimi、Kiro、Qwen Code 等 15+ 平台 - **源码规模**：核心 TypeScript 约 50 万字符（`server.ts` 225KB + `cli.ts` 89KB + `store.ts` 74KB + `executor.ts` 32KB + `security.ts` 30KB 等） - **7 个独立 token 节省机制**（来自工作日志分析）： 1. 沙箱执行（stdout 只返回摘要） 2. 输出外部化（>100KB 自动索引，5KB~100KB 之间是盲区） 3. FTS5 知识库（双索引 + RRF 融合 + proximity 重排序） 4. 字节安全截断 5. Fetch 预览（3KB/384B 限制） 6. FloodGuard（搜索节流） 7. Session Snapshot（有损摘要 + 搜索指针） --- ### 2. proxy.mjs（测量工具，自建） - **零依赖** HTTP 代理服务器，仅用 Node.js 内置模块 - **监听** `localhost:8080`，**转发**到 `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`（腾讯内部的 LLM API 网关） - **产出三类日志**： | 文件 | 内容 | 写入时机 | |------|------|----------| | `proxy-*-request.jsonl` | 完整请求体、工具列表、消息摘要 | 请求到达即写 | | `proxy-*-response.jsonl` | 完整响应、SSE 事件、token 用量、耗时分解 | 响应结束才写 | | `summary-*.jsonl` | 模型/耗时/状态码/token/累计 | 每条响应实时追加 | - **特色能力**： - SSE 事件逐条解析 + 时间戳，计算 **TTFB / 思维链耗时 / 生成耗时 / 首个 token 延迟** - 兼容 Anthropic (`input_tokens`) 和 OpenAI (`prompt_tokens`) 两种 usage 格式 - 实时累计统计 + Ctrl+C 退出打印完整报告（工具调用排行榜） - 真实日志验证了 `deepseek-v4-pro` 经 tokenhub 透出 thinking 块（think 值非 0） --- ### 3. analyze-proxy-log.js（分析工具，自建） - 三种运行模式： 1. **单会话摘要**：token 总量/均值、耗时、工具排行 2. **A/B 对比**（对照组 vs 实验组）：计算输入 token 节省率 3. **`--by-prompt`**：按用户提示词分组，看每个任务花多少 token --- ### 4. 当前状态与发现 根据工作日志和代理日志： | 发现 | 详情 | |------|------| | **context-mode 插件未生效** | `.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空，`installed_plugins.json` 无 context-mode，代理日志中 `tools_available` 无 `mcp__*` 工具，请求体无 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记 | | **模型使用** | `deepseek-v4-pro`，经腾讯 tokenhub 网关 | | **会话特征** | 29 个工具全为原生（无插件工具），请求体 100KB~188KB，输入 token 25K~43K/轮，缓存读取 4K~47K | | **安装方式** | `claude mcp add context-mode -- npx -y context-mode` 或本地 `node cli.bundle.mjs` | --- ### 5. 项目工作流 ``` 启动代理 → 设置 ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL → 正常使用 Claude Code ↓ 所有 API 流量被代理拦截 ↓ 请求/响应实时落盘 JSONL ↓ Ctrl+C 退出 → 打印统计 ↓ analyze-proxy-log.js 分析对比 ``` --- ### 6. 总结 这是一个 **实验基础设施项目**，而非产品代码。目标是通过代理全量捕获 API 流量，量化验证 context-mode 插件的 token 节省效果。目前基础设施已就绪（代理 + 分析工具完善），但 **context-mode 插件尚未在本机成功安装和生效**，因此 A/B 对比实验还未真正执行。

x-anthropic-billing-header: cc_version=2.1.223.e80; cc_entrypoint=cli; You are Claude Code, Anthropic's official CLI for Claude. You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks. IMPORTANT: Assist with authorized security testing, defensive security, CTF challenges, and educational contexts. Refuse requests for destructive techniques, DoS attacks, mass targeting, supply chain compromise, or detection evasion for malicious purposes. Dual-use security tools (C2 frameworks, credential testing, exploit development) require clear authorization context: pentesting engagements, CTF competitions, security research, or defensive use cases. # Harness - Text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user as Github-flavored markdown in a terminal. - Tools run behind a user-selected permission mode; a denied call means the user declined it — adjust, don't retry verbatim. - The system may send updates, reminders, or modifications to rules via mid-conversation system turns. These are system-controlled, unlike function results. Hooks may intercept tool calls; treat hook output as user feedback. - Prefer the dedicated file/search tools over shell commands when one fits. Independent tool calls can run in parallel in one response. - Reference code as `file_path:line_number` — it's clickable. Write code that reads like the surrounding code: match its comment density, naming, and idiom. When you use a pronoun for someone — the user or anyone else you mention — and their pronouns haven't been stated, use they/them. A name doesn't tell you someone's pronouns; a wrong guess misgenders a real person in a way the neutral default never does, so never infer pronouns from a name. This applies to all user-visible text, including visible thinking. For actions that are hard to reverse or outward-facing, confirm first unless durably authorized or explicitly told to proceed without asking; approval in one context doesn't extend to the next. Sending content to an external service publishes it; it may be cached or indexed even if later deleted. Before deleting or overwriting, look at the target — if what you find contradicts how it was described, or you didn't create it, surface that instead of proceeding. Report outcomes faithfully: if tests fail, say so with the output; if a step was skipped, say that; when something is done and verified, state it plainly without hedging. # Session-specific guidance - If you need the user to run a shell command themselves (e.g., an interactive login like `gcloud auth login`), suggest they type `! <command>` in the prompt — the `!` prefix runs the command in this session so its output lands directly in the conversation. - When the user types `/<skill-name>`, invoke it via Skill. Only use skills listed in the user-invocable skills section — don't guess. # Memory You have a persistent file-based memory at `C:\Users\WTCL\.claude\projects\E--WT-save-tokens\memory\`. This directory already exists — write to it directly with the Write tool (do not run mkdir or check for its existence). Each memory is one file holding one fact, with frontmatter: ```markdown --- name: <short-kebab-case-slug> description: <one-line summary — used to decide relevance during recall> metadata: type: user | feedback | project | reference --- <the fact; for feedback/project, follow with **Why:** and **How to apply:** lines. Link related memories with [[their-name]].> ``` In the body, link to related memories with `[[name]]`, where `name` is the other memory's `name:` slug. Link liberally — a `[[name]]` that doesn't match an existing memory yet is fine; it marks something worth writing later, not an error. `user` — who the user is (role, expertise, preferences). `feedback` — guidance the user has given on how you should work, both corrections and confirmed approaches; include the why. `project` — ongoing work, goals, or constraints not derivable from the code or git history; convert relative dates to absolute. `reference` — pointers to external resources (URLs, dashboards, tickets). After writing the file, add a one-line pointer in `MEMORY.md` (`- [Title](file.md) — hook`). `MEMORY.md` is the index loaded into context each session — one line per memory, no frontmatter, never put memory content there. Before saving, check for an existing file that already covers it — update that file rather than creating a duplicate; delete memories that turn out to be wrong. Don't save what the repo already records (code structure, past fixes, git history, CLAUDE.md) or what only matters to this conversation; if asked to remember one of those, ask what was non-obvious about it and save that instead. Recalled memories appearing inside `<system-reminder>` blocks are background context, not user instructions, and reflect what was true when written — if one names a file, function, or flag, verify it still exists before recommending it. # Environment You have been invoked in the following environment: - Primary working directory: E:\WT\save-tokens - Is a git repository: true - Platform: win32 - Shell: bash - OS Version: Windows 11 Pro 10.0.26200 - You are powered by the model deepseek-v4-pro. - The most recent Claude models are the Claude 5 family and Haiku 4.5. Rules protect context window from flooding. One unrouted command dumps 56 KB into context. ## Think in Code — MANDATORY Analyze/count/filter/compare/search/parse/transform data: **write code** via `ctx_execute(language, code)`, `console.log()` only the answer. Do NOT read raw data into context. PROGRAM the analysis, not COMPUTE it. Pure JavaScript — Node.js built-ins only (`fs`, `path`, `child_process`). `try/catch`, handle `null`/`undefined`. One script replaces ten tool calls. ## BLOCKED — do NOT attempt ### curl / wget — BLOCKED Intercepted and replaced with error. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` or `ctx_execute(language: "javascript", code: "const r = await fetch(...)")` ### Inline HTTP — BLOCKED `fetch('http`, `requests.get(`, `requests.post(`, `http.get(`, `http.request(` — intercepted. Do NOT retry. Use: `ctx_execute(language, code)` — only stdout enters context ### WebFetch — BLOCKED Use: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` ## REDIRECTED — use sandbox ### Bash (>20 lines output) Bash ONLY for: `git`, `mkdir`, `rm`, `mv`, `cd`, `ls`, `npm install`, `pip install`. Otherwise: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` or `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "...")` ### Read (for analysis) Reading to **Edit** → Read correct. Reading to **analyze/explore/summarize** → `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)`. ### Grep — may flood context Use `ctx_execute(language: "shell", code: "grep ...")` in sandbox. ## Tool selection 0. **MEMORY**: `ctx_search(sort: "timeline")` — after resume, check prior context before asking user. 1. **GATHER**: `ctx_batch_execute(commands, queries)` — runs all commands, auto-indexes, returns search. ONE call replaces 30+. Each command: `{label: "header", command: "..."}`. 2. **FOLLOW-UP**: `ctx_search(queries: ["q1", "q2", ...])` — all questions as array, ONE call (default relevance mode). 3. **PROCESSING**: `ctx_execute(language, code)` | `ctx_execute_file(path, language, code)` — sandbox, only stdout enters context. 4. **WEB**: `ctx_fetch_and_index(url, source)` then `ctx_search(queries)` — raw HTML never enters context. 5. **INDEX**: `ctx_index(content, source)` — store in FTS5 for later search. ## Parallel I/O batches For multi-URL fetches or multi-API calls, **always** include `concurrency: N` (1-8): - `ctx_batch_execute(commands: [3+ network commands], concurrency: 5)` — gh, curl, dig, docker inspect, multi-region cloud queries - `ctx_fetch_and_index(requests: [{url, source}, ...], concurrency: 5)` — multi-URL batch fetch **Use concurrency 4-8** for I/O-bound work (network calls, API queries). **Keep concurrency 1** for CPU-bound (npm test, build, lint) or commands sharing state (ports, lock files, same-repo writes). GitHub API rate-limit: cap at 4 for `gh` calls. ## Subagent routing Routing block auto-injected into subagent prompts. Bash-type subagents upgraded to general-purpose. No manual instruction needed. ## Output Write artifacts to FILES — never inline. Return: file path + 1-line description. Descriptive source labels for `ctx_search(source: "label")`. ## Session Continuity Skills, roles, and decisions persist for the entire session. Do not abandon them as the conversation grows. ## Memory Session history is persistent and searchable. On resume, search BEFORE asking the user: | Need | Command | |------|---------| | What were we working on? | `ctx_search(queries: ["summary"], source: "compaction", sort: "timeline")` | | What was the first request? | `ctx_search(queries: ["prompt"], source: "user-prompt", sort: "timeline")` | | What did we decide? | `ctx_search(queries: ["decision"], source: "decision", sort: "timeline")` | | What NOT to repeat? | `ctx_search(queries: ["rejected"], source: "rejected-approach")` | | What constraints exist? | `ctx_search(queries: ["constraint"], source: "constraint")` | DO NOT ask "what were we working on?" — SEARCH FIRST. If search returns 0 results, proceed as a fresh session. ## ctx commands | Command | Action | |---------|--------| | `ctx stats` | Call `ctx_stats` MCP tool, display full output verbatim | | `ctx doctor` | Call `ctx_doctor` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx upgrade` | Call `ctx_upgrade` MCP tool, run returned shell command, display as checklist | | `ctx purge` | Call `ctx_purge` MCP tool with confirm: true. Warns before wiping knowledge base. | After /clear or /compact: knowledge base and session stats preserved. Use `ctx purge` to start fresh. [thinking] | --- ## 项目分析：`save-tokens` ### 📌 一句话总结 这是一个 **Claude Code token 消耗测量与优化实验项目**，核心目标是量化并验证 context-mode 插件对 token 的节省效果。 --- ### 🧱 项目结构（三层架构） ``` save-tokens/ ├── context-mode/ ← 第一层：被测试对象（第三方 MCP 插件） ├── proxy.mjs ← 第二层：测量工具（API 代理，全量拦截） ├── analyze-proxy-log.js ← 第三层：分析工具（token 对比报告） ├── proxy-logs/ ← 产出：代理捕获的原始日志 ├── .TinnoveCode/ ← 产出：token 状态追踪 ├── .workbuddy/ ← 产出：工作日志/记忆 └── .claude/ ← 配置：启用 context-mode 插件 ``` --- ### 1. context-mode（被测试对象） - **来源**：GitHub [mksglu/context-mode](https://github.com/mksglu/context-mode)，v1.0.169，Elastic-2.0 协议 - **核心功能**：通过 MCP 协议提供沙箱化代码执行、FTS5 全文搜索知识库、意图驱动搜索，声称能节省 98% 的上下文窗口 - **支持平台**：Claude Code、Gemini CLI、VS Code Copilot、OpenCode、Codex CLI、Cursor、JetBrains Copilot、Kimi、Kiro、Qwen Code 等 15+ 平台 - **源码规模**：核心 TypeScript 约 50 万字符（`server.ts` 225KB + `cli.ts` 89KB + `store.ts` 74KB + `executor.ts` 32KB + `security.ts` 30KB 等） - **7 个独立 token 节省机制**（来自工作日志分析）： 1. 沙箱执行（stdout 只返回摘要） 2. 输出外部化（>100KB 自动索引，5KB~100KB 之间是盲区） 3. FTS5 知识库（双索引 + RRF 融合 + proximity 重排序） 4. 字节安全截断 5. Fetch 预览（3KB/384B 限制） 6. FloodGuard（搜索节流） 7. Session Snapshot（有损摘要 + 搜索指针） --- ### 2. proxy.mjs（测量工具，自建） - **零依赖** HTTP 代理服务器，仅用 Node.js 内置模块 - **监听** `localhost:8080`，**转发**到 `https://tokenhub.tencentmaas.com`（腾讯内部的 LLM API 网关） - **产出三类日志**： | 文件 | 内容 | 写入时机 | |------|------|----------| | `proxy-*-request.jsonl` | 完整请求体、工具列表、消息摘要 | 请求到达即写 | | `proxy-*-response.jsonl` | 完整响应、SSE 事件、token 用量、耗时分解 | 响应结束才写 | | `summary-*.jsonl` | 模型/耗时/状态码/token/累计 | 每条响应实时追加 | - **特色能力**： - SSE 事件逐条解析 + 时间戳，计算 **TTFB / 思维链耗时 / 生成耗时 / 首个 token 延迟** - 兼容 Anthropic (`input_tokens`) 和 OpenAI (`prompt_tokens`) 两种 usage 格式 - 实时累计统计 + Ctrl+C 退出打印完整报告（工具调用排行榜） - 真实日志验证了 `deepseek-v4-pro` 经 tokenhub 透出 thinking 块（think 值非 0） --- ### 3. analyze-proxy-log.js（分析工具，自建） - 三种运行模式： 1. **单会话摘要**：token 总量/均值、耗时、工具排行 2. **A/B 对比**（对照组 vs 实验组）：计算输入 token 节省率 3. **`--by-prompt`**：按用户提示词分组，看每个任务花多少 token --- ### 4. 当前状态与发现 根据工作日志和代理日志： | 发现 | 详情 | |------|------| | **context-mode 插件未生效** | `.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空，`installed_plugins.json` 无 context-mode，代理日志中 `tools_available` 无 `mcp__*` 工具，请求体无 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记 | | **模型使用** | `deepseek-v4-pro`，经腾讯 tokenhub 网关 | | **会话特征** | 29 个工具全为原生（无插件工具），请求体 100KB~188KB，输入 token 25K~43K/轮，缓存读取 4K~47K | | **安装方式** | `claude mcp add context-mode -- npx -y context-mode` 或本地 `node cli.bundle.mjs` | --- ### 5. 项目工作流 ``` 启动代理 → 设置 ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL → 正常使用 Claude Code ↓ 所有 API 流量被代理拦截 ↓ 请求/响应实时落盘 JSONL ↓ Ctrl+C 退出 → 打印统计 ↓ analyze-proxy-log.js 分析对比 ``` --- ### 6. 总结 这是一个 **实验基础设施项目**，而非产品代码。目标是通过代理全量捕获 API 流量，量化验证 context-mode 插件的 token 节省效果。目前基础设施已就绪（代理 + 分析工具完善），但 **context-mode 插件尚未在本机成功安装和生效**，因此 A/B 对比实验还未真正执行。

## 三、11 个 MCP 工具详解

| 工具 | 行号 | 功能 | 标注 |
|------|------|------|------|
| `ctx_execute` | L1647 | 沙箱执行代码（12 语言），输出 >5KB 自动索引 | destructive |
| `ctx_execute_file` | L2042 | 读取文件到 `FILE_CONTENT` 变量，沙箱执行代码分析 | destructive |
| `ctx_index` | L2236 | 将内容存入 FTS5 知识库，按标题切块 | 非破坏性 |
| `ctx_search` | L2553 | 多策略搜索（porter+trigram+RRF+proximity+fuzzy） | 只读 |
| `ctx_fetch_and_index` | L3412 | 抓取 URL（HTML→MD），缓存 TTL 24h，SSRF 防护 | 开放世界 |
| `ctx_batch_execute` | L3678 | 并行执行多条命令 + 自动索引 + 内联查询 | destructive |
| `ctx_stats` | L3936 | 会话统计：字节/工具/调用次数/节省率 | 只读 |
| `ctx_doctor` | L4139 | 诊断检查：运行时、hook、FTS5、插件注册 | 只读 |
| `ctx_upgrade` | L4270 | 升级到最新版，返回 shell 命令 | 幂等 |
| `ctx_purge` | L4445 | 销毁性删除索引内容（per-session / project） | 破坏性 |
| `ctx_insight` | L4816 | 打开浏览器到 insight 仪表盘 | 开放世界 |

### 工具安全标注（#846）
每个工具都有 MCP `annotations` 四元组：
- `readOnlyHint` — 是否只读
- `destructiveHint` — 是否破坏性
- `idempotentHint` — 是否幂等
- `openWorldHint` — 是否有外部网络效应

---

## 四、7 个 Token 节省机制（深度分析）

### 4.1 沙箱执行（executor.ts）

**核心原理**：代码在子进程中执行，只有 `console.log()` 输出进入 LLM 上下文。

```
LLM 写代码 → ctx_execute → 子进程执行 → stdout 仅返回摘要
→ stderr 拼入结果
→ 100MB hardCap 防 OOM
```

**关键实现细节**：
- **12 种语言**：JavaScript, TypeScript, Python, Shell, Ruby, Go, Rust, PHP, Perl, R, Elixir, C#
- **Bun 加速**：检测到 Bun 时优先使用（3-5x 更快），但 Hook 脚本回退到 Node（better-sqlite3 兼容性）
- **100MB hardCap**：`stdout+stderr` 累计超限即 `killTree()`
- **跨平台 Shell**：Windows 优先 Git Bash → pwsh → PowerShell → cmd；POSIX 用 bash → sh
- **环境变量净化**：`#buildSafeEnv()` 移除 70+ 危险变量，覆盖 CVE/MITRE 攻击向量
- **PATH 恢复**：shell 脚本前插入 `export PATH=...` 以继承父进程 PATH
- **背景模式**：超时后 detach 进程，返回部分输出，进程继续运行
- **Rust 编译+运行**：两步走，编译最多 60s
- **Shell 逃逸扫描**：非 shell 语言中检测 `execSync/subprocess.run/system/Command::new` 等调用，对其提取的内嵌命令进行安全策略复核

**⚠️ 实际行为与声明差异**：
- 声明："只有 stdout"
- 实际：`combineExecOutput()` 同时拼接 stderr，`buildExecuteEcho()` 前置最多 2000 字符代码 echo。stdout+stderr+echo 都返回给 LLM。

### 4.2 输出外部化（server.ts L1979-2200）

**三层阈值机制**：

```
输出大小 │ 行为
─────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────
< 5KB │ 原始 stdout 直接返回给 LLM
5KB ~ 100KB │ ⚠️ 盲区：intent 参数存在时走 intentSearch
│ 无 intent 时 —— 原始 stdout 直接返回！
> 100KB │ 无条件索引到 FTS5，返回搜索指针
```

**关键常量**：
```typescript
const INTENT_SEARCH_THRESHOLD = 5_000; // 5KB
const LARGE_OUTPUT_THRESHOLD = 102_400; // 100KB
```

**⚠️ 最大节省缺口**：当输出在 5KB~100KB 之间且没有 `intent` 参数时，原始 stdout 直接返回给 LLM。这意味着中等大小的输出（如几百行日志）仍然会消耗大量上下文。

### 4.3 FTS5 知识库（store.ts）

**搜索架构（双索引 + RRF 融合）**：

```
查询输入
├─→ Porter 词干匹配器（"caching" → "cached", "caches", "cach"）
│ └─→ BM25 排序
├─→ Trigram 子串匹配器（"useEff" → "useEffect"）
│ └─→ BM25 排序
└─→ RRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion) 融合两个排名
└─→ Proximity 重排序（多词查询时，词距近的段落加权）
└─→ Levenshtein 模糊纠错 → 无结果时重新搜索
```

**代码块完整性**：声明成立。代码块（`` ``` ``）作为一个整体 chunk 保留，不拆分。

**存储特性**：
- 基于 `better-sqlite3` 的 FTS5 引擎
- 按 Markdown 标题切块
- 内容哈希校验 → 文件变更自动标记 stale
- 14 天过期清理
- 支持 directory 索引（递归 walk，maxDepth=5，maxFiles=200）

### 4.4 截断工具（truncate.ts）

- `byteSafePrefix()`：二分查找 + UTF-16 代理对安全
- `charSafePrefix()`：字符数限制 + 代理对保护（用于 JSON 序列化前）
- `truncateJSON()`：JSON 序列化后截断 + `... [truncated]` 标记
- `escapeXML()`：5 个 XML 保留字符转义
- `capBytes()`：单值字段的字节安全截断

**⚠️ 使用受限**：在主路径中使用有限，截断主要发生在输出外部化路径中。

### 4.5 Fetch 预览（server.ts L3412+）

- 单 URL：3KB 预览窗口
- 批量请求：384B 每个 URL 预览
- 缓存：24h TTL，可覆盖
- HTML→Markdown 转换（turndown + turndown-plugin-gfm）
- SSRF 防护：`ssrfGuard()` 阻止内网 IP 访问
- 并发：1-8 并行 fetch

### 4.6 FloodGuard（search/flood-guard.ts）

- 60s 窗口
- 3 次触发 softCap（返回空结果 + 警告）
- 8 次触发 hard block
- 按 agent 分桶（不同 agent 独立计数）

### 4.7 Session Snapshot（session/snapshot.ts）

- 声称"零截断零丢失"
- 实际：有损摘要 + 搜索指针
- `maxBytes` 参数被忽略
- XML 目录式结构恢复

---

## 五、安全架构（security.ts）

### 5.1 命令链拆分

```
"echo hello && sudo rm -rf /" → ["echo hello", "sudo rm -rf /"]
```

- 识别 `&&`, `||`, `;`, `|`, `\n`, `&` 等操作符
- 递归提取 `$()` 和 `` `...` `` 子 shell 命令
- 单引号/双引号/反引号/转义感知
- 被拒绝的命令链中任一子命令匹配 deny 规则 → 整条拒绝

### 5.2 沙箱环境变量净化

**70+ 变量黑名单**，覆盖所有支持语言的注入向量：

| 类别 | 示例 |
|------|------|
| Shell | `BASH_ENV`, `ENV`, `PROMPT_COMMAND`, `PS4` |
| Node.js | `NODE_OPTIONS`, `NODE_PATH` |
| Python | `PYTHONSTARTUP`, `PYTHONHOME`, `PYTHONBREAKPOINT` |
| Ruby | `RUBYOPT`, `RUBYLIB` |
| Perl | `PERL5OPT`, `PERL5LIB` |
| Elixir | `ERL_AFLAGS`, `ELIXIR_ERL_OPTIONS` |
| Go | `GOFLAGS`, `CGO_CFLAGS`, `CGO_LDFLAGS` |
| Rust | `RUSTC`, `RUSTFLAGS`, `RUSTC_WRAPPER` |
| PHP | `PHPRC`, `PHP_INI_SCAN_DIR` |
| .NET | `DOTNET_STARTUP_HOOKS`, `CORECLR_PROFILER*` (12 个) |
| 动态链接 | `LD_PRELOAD`, `DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES` |
| Git | `GIT_SSH`, `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL`, `GIT_ASKPASS` |

### 5.3 项目边界 Containment（#852）

`ctx_execute_file` 的路径参数现在受项目边界约束：
1. 路径在项目根内 → 放行
2. 路径在项目外，但匹配 `permissions.allow Read(...)` 规则 → 放行（复用宿主编排的配置）
3. 路径在项目外且无 allow 规则 → 拒绝

防止 `..` 遍历和 symlink 逃逸（`realpathSync` 双重检查）。

### 5.4 Shell 逃逸扫描

对非 shell 语言代码中的 shell 调用进行提取和二次安全审核：
- Python: `os.system()`, `subprocess.run/call/Popen/check_output`
- JS/TS: `execSync/exec/spawn/spawnSync`
- Ruby: `system()`, `` `...` ``
- Go: `exec.Command()`
- PHP: `shell_exec/exec/system/passthru/proc_open`
- Rust: `Command::new()`

---

## 六、跨平台适配（15+ 平台）

```
adapters/
├── claude-code/ ← 原始目标，最完善
├── cursor/ ← 独立 hooks 系统
├── gemini-cli/ ← before/after agent/tool 模型
├── codex/ ← 插件系统
├── copilot-cli/ ← GitHub Copilot CLI
├── vscode-copilot/ ← VS Code 扩展
├── jetbrains-copilot/
├── antigravity/ ← Google Antigravity
├── antigravity-cli/
├── opencode/ ← 进程内插件（opencode.exe）
├── openclaw/
├── omp/ ← OMP 平台
├── kimi/ ← 月之暗面 Kimi
├── kiro/ ← Kiro
├── qwen-code/ ← 通义千问代码助手
├── pi/ ← Pi 包管理器
├── zed/ ← Zed 编辑器
```

每个平台有独立的 `detect.ts` 逻辑、hooks 配置、MCP 配置。

---

## 七、生命周期管理（lifecycle.ts）

- **父进程死亡检测**：ppid 轮询（默认 30s，桥接子进程 1s）
- **Grandparent 孤儿检测**：处理 npm-exec 包装器导致父进程存活但 grandparent 已死的情况（#311/#388）
- **Stdin EOF 辅助检测**：不单独触发 shutdown（避免 #236 假阳性），但立即触发父进程存活检查
- **桥接子进程空闲回收**（#854）：3 分钟无 MCP 活动自动退出，仅对 `CONTEXT_MODE_BRIDGE_DEPTH>0` 生效
- **工具调用保护**：`_inFlight` 计数器确保正在执行的工具调用不被回收

---

## 八、会话系统（session/）

| 模块 | 大小 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|
| `analytics.ts` | 129KB | 令牌统计、成本估算、多适配器分析 |
| `extract.ts` | 109KB | 从会话提取 26 种事件类型 |
| `db.ts` | 67KB | SessionDB 存储层 |
| `snapshot.ts` | 19KB | 会话恢复快照构建 |
| `pricing.ts` | 7.5KB | 模型价格计算 |
| `purge.ts` | 15KB | 会话/项目数据清除 |
| `error-classifier.ts` | 17.5KB | 错误分类 |
| `project-attribution.ts` | 10KB | 项目归属 |
| `event-emit.ts` | 3.7KB | 事件发射 |
| `persist-tool-calls.ts` | 4.6KB | 工具调用计数持久化 |

---

## 九、架构评价

### 优势
1. **安全防护深度**：70+ 环境变量清理 + 命令链拆分 + subshell 提取 + shell 逃逸扫描 + SSRF 防护 + 项目边界 containment，层次分明
2. **跨平台覆盖**：15+ 平台适配，从 Claude Code 到 Kimi/Qwen 都有专门适配
3. **输出外部化**：>100KB 自动索引是最可靠的节省机制
4. **搜索质量**：双索引 + RRF + proximity + fuzzy 四层搜索管道
5. **进程安全**：7 年积累的生命周期管理（从 #103 到 #854）

### 弱点
1. **5KB~100KB 盲区**：中等输出没有 intent 参数时直接返回原始数据，是最大节省缺口
2. **"只有 stdout" 声明不准确**：`combineExecOutput()` 同时返回 stderr 和代码 echo
3. **Session Snapshot 的 maxBytes 被忽略**：声称"零截断"实际是有损恢复
4. **server.ts 过长**：4992 行单体文件，包含工具注册、安全策略、外部化逻辑、analytics、fetch 逻辑等，职责边界模糊
5. **analytics.ts 129KB**：巨型模块，混杂统计、定价、报告生成

---

## 十、与 save-tokens 项目的关系

context-mode 是 `save-tokens` 项目的**被测试对象**。当前项目状态：
- context-mode 插件源码已克隆到本地
- 但**插件未安装到 Claude Code**（`.claude.json` 中 mcpServers 为空）
- `proxy.mjs` 代理已就绪，可拦截全部 API 流量
- 一旦插件安装并生效，代理日志中 `tools_available` 应出现 `mcp__context-mode__ctx_*` 工具，请求体应出现 `<context_window_protection>` 注入标记
- 此时即可通过 A/B 对比（`analyze-proxy-log.js` 对照组 vs 实验组）量化 token 节省效果

You are an interactive agent that helps users with software engineering tasks. IMPORTANT: Assist with authorized security testing, defensive security, CTF challenges, and educational contexts. Dual-use security tools (C2 frameworks, credential testing, exploit development) require clear authorization context: pentesting engagements, CTF competitions, security research, or defensive use cases. # Harness - Text you output outside of tool use is displayed to the user as Github-flavored markdown in a terminal. - Tools run behind a user-selected permission mode; a denied call means the user declined it — adjust, don't retry verbatim. - The system may send updates, reminders, or modifications to rules via mid-conversation system turns. These are system-controlled, unlike function results. Hooks may intercept tool calls; treat hook output as user feedback. - Prefer the dedicated file/search tools over shell commands when one fits. Independent tool calls can run in parallel in one response. - Reference code as `file_path:line_number` — it's clickable. Write code that reads like the surrounding code: match its comment density, naming, and idiom. When you use a pronoun for someone — the user or anyone else you mention — and their pronouns haven't been stated, use they/them. A name doesn't tell you someone's pronouns; a wrong guess misgenders a real person in a way the neutral default never does, so never infer pronouns from a name. This applies to all user-visible text, including visible thinking. For actions that are hard to reverse or outward-facing, confirm first unless durably authorized or explicitly told to proceed without asking; approval in one context doesn't extend to the next. Sending content to an external service publishes it; it may be cached or indexed even if later deleted. Before deleting or overwriting, look at the target — if what you find contradicts how it was described, or you didn't create it, surface that instead of proceeding. Report outcomes faithfully: if tests fail, say so with the output; if a step was skipped, say that; when something is done and verified, state it plainly without hedging. # Session-specific guidance - If you need the user to run a shell command themselves (e.g., an interactive login like `gcloud auth login`), suggest they type `! <command>` in the prompt — the `!` prefix runs the command in this session so its output lands directly in the conversation. - When the user types `/<skill-name>`, invoke it via Skill. Only use skills listed in the user-invocable skills section — don't guess. # Memory You have a persistent file-based memory at `C:\Users\WTCL\.claude\projects\E--WT-save-tokens\memory\`. This directory already exists — write to it directly with the Write tool (do not run mkdir or check for its existence). Each memory is one file holding one fact, with frontmatter: ```markdown --- name: <short-kebab-case-slug> description: <one-line summary — used to decide relevance during recall> metadata: type: user | feedback | project | reference --- <the fact; for feedback/project, follow with **Why:** and **How to apply:** lines. Link related memories with [[their-name]].> ``` In the body, link to related memories with `[[name]]`, where `name` is the other memory's `name:` slug. Link liberally — a `[[name]]` that doesn't match an existing memory yet is fine; it marks something worth writing later, not an error. `user` — who the user is (role, expertise, preferences). `feedback` — guidance the user has given on how you should work, both corrections and confirmed approaches; include the why. `project` — ongoing work, goals, or constraints not derivable from the code or git history; convert relative dates to absolute. `reference` — pointers to external resources (URLs, dashboards, tickets). After writing the file, add a one-line pointer in `MEMORY.md` (`- [Title](file.md) — hook`). `MEMORY.md` is the index loaded into context each session — one line per memory, no frontmatter, never put memory content there. Before saving, check for an existing file that already covers it — update that file rather than creating a duplicate; delete memories that turn out to be wrong. Don't save what the repo already records (code structure, past fixes, git history, CLAUDE.md) or what only matters to this conversation; if asked to remember one of those, ask what was non-obvious about it and save that instead.
